root@256ac3b8defa:/app# python3 -c "
> import itertools
> import sys
> # Generate all possible passwords of length 23
> # Format: 8XD + 17 chars + W54
> # Each char from A-Z0-9
> chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789'
> # We'll generate in chunks of 10,000
> chunk = 10000
> with open('recovered_passwords.txt', 'w') as f:
>     for i in range(0, len(chars)**17, chunk):
>         for combo in itertools.product(chars, repeat=17):
>             # Convert to string
>             password = '8XD' + ''.join(combo) + 'W54'
>             f.write(password + '\n')
>             # If we have enough, break and start next chunk
>             if i + chunk >= len(chars)**17:
>                 break
>         # Break out of the outer loop after writing chunk
>         break
> "
wc -l recovered_passwords.txt
head -5 recovered_passwords.txt
tail -5 recovered_passwords.txt
ls -l recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'file not found'
wc -l recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'no file'
python3 -c "
import sys
chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789'
print('Alphabet length:', len(chars))
print('Combinations:', len(chars)**17)
"
python3 -c "
import re
with open('/app/varsea/disks/sub1/subA/subAlpha/ae3f4c.dat', 'rb') as f:
    data = f.read()
# Extract printable characters (ASCII 32-126)
printable = [chr(b) for b in data if 32 <= b <= 126]
print('Printable chars:', printable)
# Maybe the password is a substring
password_pattern = re.compile(r'8XD[^\s]{17}W54')
for match in password_pattern.finditer(''.join(printable)):
    print('Match:', match.group())
"
grep -r -l 'PASSWORD=' /app 2>/dev/null
find /app -type f -name '*.log' -exec grep -l 'PASSWORD=' {} \; 2>/dev/null
find /app -type f -name '*.txt' -exec grep -l 'PASSWORD=' {} \; 2>/dev/null

ls -l /app/recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'recovered_passwords.txt not found'
wc -l /app/recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'no lines'
python3 -c "
import re
with open('/app/varsea/disks/sub1/subA/subAlpha/ae3f4c.dat', 'rb') as f:
    data = f.read()
printable = [chr(b) for b in data if 32 <= b <= 126]
print('Printable characters count:', len(printable))
print('First 200 printable chars:', ''.join(printable)[:200])
# Search for pattern
pattern = re.compile(r'8XD[^\s]{17}W54')
for match in pattern.finditer(''.join(printable)):
    print('Match found:', match.group())
"
grep -r -l 'PASSWORD=' /app 2>/dev/null
find /app -type f -name '*.log' -exec grep -l 'PASSWORD=' {} \; 2>/dev/null
find /app -type f -name '*.txt' -exec grep -l 'PASSWORD=' {} \; 2>/dev/null

ls -l /app/recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'recovered_passwords.txt not found'
wc -l /app/recovered_passwords.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 'no lines'
